do echo ===$g===; sed -n \'1,180p\' /tmp/generate_source_s3/$g/agent_output/model_patch.diff; done"},\n        "tool_output": "import json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport socket\nimport http.client\nimport time\n\n# Starting task agent (A_0) for the ARC-AGI-2 domain: one direct model response per attempt.\n# forward() makes two independent plain attempts (the budget is 2 model calls and 2 answers per\n# test input) and returns both parsed grids.\n\n\nclass _UnixConn(http.client.HTTPConnection):\n    def __init__(self, path, timeout=3600):\n        super().__init__("localhost", timeout=timeout)\n        self._path = path\n\n    def connect(self):\n        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)\n        s.settimeout(self.timeout)\n        s.connect(self._path)\n        self.sock = s\n\n\ndef call_model(prompt):\n    """One model call through the budget gateway (model and reasoning effort are fixed there)."""\n    path = os.environ.get("GW_SOCKET", "/run/arcgw/gw.sock")\n    body = json.dumps({"token": os.environ.get("GW_TOKEN", ""),\n                       "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]}).encode()\n    last = None\n    for attempt in range(3):\n        try:\n            c = _UnixConn(path)\n            c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\n            r = c.getresponse()\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\n            c.close()\n            if r.status == 200:\n                return data.get("text", "")\n            last = f"gateway HTTP {r.status}: {data.get(\'error\')}"\n            if r.status in (402, 403, 429):\n                break\n        except Exception as e:\n            last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"\n        time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\n    raise RuntimeError(last)\n\n\ndef grid_to_text(grid):\n    return "\\n".join(" ".join(str(v) for v in row) for row in grid)\n\n\ndef build_prompt(inputs):\n    parts = [\n        "You are solving an ARC-AGI puzzle. Each grid is a rectangular 2D array of integers 0-9 "\n        "(each integer is a color). The demonstration pairs below share one hidden transformation rule "\n        "that maps each input grid to its output grid. Infer the rule and apply it to the test input.",\n        "Compare every example carefully: identify which objects, colors, shapes, positions, counts, and "\n        "grid dimensions change or stay fixed. record["valid"]:\n                completed.setdefault(scope, []).append(record)\n            else:\n                incomplete.append((domain, record))\n\n    lines = ["Prior evaluation evidence and reports before making performance claims.")\n    for (domain, sample_count), records in sorted(completed.items()):\n        records.sort(key=lambda item: (item["score"], str(item["generation"])), reverse=True)\n        count_text = "unknown sample count" if sample_count is None else f"{sample_count} test inputs/tasks"\n        top_records = records[:max_candidates]\n        # Keep the starting agent visible even when it is not among the top candidates.\n        baseline = next((record for record in records if record["generation"] == "initial"), None)\n        if baseline is not None and baseline not in top_records:\n            top_records.append(baseline)\n        entries = [\n            f"gen_{record[\'generation\']}={record[\'score\']:.4f} (parent {record[\'parent\']})"\n            for record in top_records\n        ]\n        lines.append(f"- {domain}, {count_text}: best completed candidates and baseline: " + "; ".join(entries) + ".")\n\n        report = records[0]["report"]\n        failures = report.get("question_ids_failed", []) if isinstance(report, dict) else []\n        if not failures and isinstance(report, dict):\n            task_scores = report.get("per_task_score")\n            if isinstance(task_scores, dict):\n                failures = [\n                    task_id for task_id, value in task_scores.items()\n                    if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and value < 1\n                ]\n        if failures:\n            lines.append(\n                "  Best candidate\'s reported failing task IDs: "\n                + ", ".join(map(str, failures[:12])) + "."\n            )\n\n    if incomplete:\n        descriptions = []\n        for domain, record in incomplete[:8]:\n            status = "partial evaluation" if not record["complete"] else "invalid parent"\n            descriptions.append(\n                f"{domain} gen_{record[\'generation\']} ({status}, score {record[\'score\']:.4f})"\n            )\n        lines.append("- Excluded from best-candidate comparisons: " + "; ".join(descriptions) + ".")\n    return "\\n".join(lines)\n\n\nclass MetaAgent(AgentSystem):\n    def forward(self, repo_path, eval_path, iterations_left=None):\n        """Recursively improve the agent using repository code and evaluation evidence."""\n        evidence = _evaluation_summary(eval_path)\n        instruction = f"Modify any part of the codebase at `{repo_path}`."\n        instruction += (\n            f"\\n\\nThe repository\'s README.md describes the goal. "\n            "Use the tools to inspect the code and make your changes; a response without a tool call ends the session, so only reply with a tool call or DONE when finished."\n            "\\n\\nUse this automatically compiled evaluation summary as a starting point, then verify the relevant reports, metadata, and patches yourself:\\n"\n            f"{evidence}\\n\\n"\n            "Choose changes based on evidence, not generation number or recency. Inspect the best candidate\'s patch and its failures, preserve improvements that are supported by results, and propose a focused change aimed at a demonstrated weakness. Do not assume a prompt change helped unless a comparable evaluation supports it."\n        )\n        if iterations_left is not None:\n            instruction += f"\\nThere are {iterations_left} future generation iterations remaining."\n\n        return chat_with_agent(\n            instruction,\n            model=self.model,\n            msg_history=[],\n            logging=self.log,\n            tools_available="all",\n        )\n\n--- evaluator input selection/scoring ---\nimport argparse\nimport os\nfrom datetime import datetime\nimport shutil\nimport docker\n\nfrom utils.constants import REPO_NAME\nfrom utils.docker_utils import (\n    build_container,\n    cleanup_container,\n    copy_from_container,\n    log_container_output,\n    safe_log,\n    setup_logger,\n)\nfrom utils.domain_utils import get_domain_test_subset\nfrom utils.gl_utils import apply_diffs_container, setup_initial_gen\n\n\ndef run_eval(\n    output_dir,\n    domain,\n    run_id=None,\n    num_samples=-1,\n    num_workers=5,\n    subset="",\n    patch_files=None,\n    copy_root_dir=None,\n):\n    # Setup\n    parent_output_dir = (\n        os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "outputs/") if output_dir is None else output_dir\n    )\n    output_dir = os.path.join(parent_output_dir, f"{run_id}/")\n    if os.path.exists(output_dir):\n        # If output_dir already exists, it means that the evaluation was already done, so skip\n        print(f"Output directory {output_dir} already exists, skipping.")\n        return\n    root_dir, root_commit = setup_initial_gen(\n        output_dir, [domain], copy_root_dir=copy_root_dir,\n        subsets=[subset], resume=False, copy_eval=False,\n    )\n    logger = setup_logger(os.path.join(output_dir, "run_eval.log"))\n\n    # Save args\n    args_dict = locals()\n    args_str = ", ".join([f"{k}={v}" for k, v in args_dict.items()])\n    safe_log(f"Args: {args_str}\\n")\n\n    # Create and start the Docker container\n    image_name = f"{REPO_NAME}"\n    container_name = f"{REPO_NAME}-{domain}-eval-container-{run_id}"\n    client = docker.DockerClient()\n    container = build_container(\n        client, root_dir, image_name, container_name, domains=[domain],\n    )\n\n    container.start()\n    container_output_folder = "/tmp/"\n\n    try:\n        # 6h timeout\n            "python",\n            "-m",\n            f"domains.harness",\n            "--output_dir",\n            container_output_folder,\n            "--run_id",\n            eval_run_id,\n            "--domain",\n            domain,\n            "--num_samples",\n            str(num_samples),\n            "--num_workers",\n            str(num_workers),\n            "--subset",\n            subset,\n        ]\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(cmd=command, workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}")\n        log_container_output(exec_result)\n        command = [\n            "timeout",\n            "3600",  # one or more domains\n        required=True,\n        help="One or more domains to evaluate (must be from the allowed list)",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--num_samples",\n        type=int,\n        default=-1,\n        help="Number of samples to evaluate, -1 for all",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--num_workers", type=int, default=10, help="Number of parallel workers"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--patch_files", type=str, nargs="+", default=None, help="Patch files to apply"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--repeat", type=int, default=1, help="Number of times to repeat this eval"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--copy_root_dir", type=str, default=None, help="Path to a root dir to copy"\n    )\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    base_run_id = args.run_id\n    base_run_id = (\n        datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")\n        if base_run_id is None\n        else base_run_id\n    )\n    test_subsets = [get_domain_test_subset(d) for d in args.domains]\n\n    for domain, subset in zip(args.domains, test_subsets):\n        for i in range(args.repeat):\n            run_id = f"{base_run_id}_{domain}_{i}"\n            run_eval(\n                output_dir=args.output_dir,\n                domain=domain,\n                run_id=run_id,\n                num_samples=args.num_samples,\n                num_workers=args.num_workers,\n                subset=subset,\n                patch_files=args.patch_files,\n                copy_root_dir=args.copy_root_dir,\n            )\n# ARC-AGI-2 domain (modelled on the IMO grading domain).\nQUESTION_ID = "task_id"\nGROUND_TRUTH_KEY = None  #
